Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the oceanography space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 3 and the convergence rate is O(144/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the oceanography space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 3 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 144. QED.
